{"schema": "training-reflection-handoff-3", "materials": [{"role": "training_task", "content": "A transfer with a warm progress source shows no initial progress until a later\npacket arrives. Restore the initial progress update without changing the public\ntransfer, retry, navigation, cleanup, or audit behavior. Preserve the ordinary\nstreaming path and subsequent uses. The queue-record format is outside this fix.\n", "artifact": "inputs/task.md", "sha256": "e12b81b374339567658df7bc8ebfff06ce27fc46cb124f5366b4bf684c373d52"}, {"role": "training_document", "content": "# Transfer retry panel\n\nThis frontend workspace selects files, starts transfers, and shows queued status.\nRun with Node v16.20.2; there are no dependencies. `src/workspace.js` exports\n`createTransferWorkspace({ transport, onCancel, record, showQueued })`. Each\nworkspace has independent visit and attempt counters. A `select(file)` call\nopens a new visit, including when the exact same file object is selected again.\n`retry()` accepts one retry for the current visit; `hide()` closes its panel;\n`dispose()` ends the workspace. `snapshot()` exposes current ownership.\n\nRetry already cancels the previous transfer before enqueueing an accepted retry. Preserve that behavior during progress maintenance.\nAn accepted retry always belongs to the file and visit present at entry, creates\nexactly one new attempt, and remains in the queue record even if cancellation\ncauses navigation. Attempt IDs increase once per enqueued command within each\nworkspace; navigation must not retarget an accepted retry to the newly selected\nfile or drop its command. Queueing and recording are synchronous.\n\nCancellation uses a native `AbortController`: `abort()` synchronously calls the\nhost's `onCancel` observer before returning. That observer may select another\nfile, hide the panel, or hide and reopen the identical file object. New selection\nimmediately starts its own transfer. The newer visit must retain its view and\nhandle when the older retry resumes. Queued notices are visible output: a notice\nfrom a retired visit must not be sent, including after reopening the same file.\nNormal retries in a current visit still issue one queued notice each.\n\nThe accepted old retry must still enqueue and record once after such navigation.\nIts newly created transfer is canceled immediately if its visit has ended; this\ndoes not remove its retained queue record. There must be no remaining active\ntransfer from that old visit. The new visit remains able to retry and hide;\nhiding cancels its owned handle and repeated cancellation is idempotent.\nSelection and hiding may themselves cancel an old handle and synchronously\nreceive newer navigation; the newer navigation keeps ownership. A hidden panel\naccepts no retry. Workspace disposal cancels every remaining transfer and is\nidempotent. Disposing one workspace cannot affect another.\n\n`transport({file, attemptId, signal})` starts a pending physical transfer and\nobserves the native signal. `record` and `showQueued` are passive synchronous\nsinks. Every queue record remains exactly `{fileId, attemptId, visitId, kind}`;\n`kind` is `select` or `retry`. Adding `byteCount` from `file.size` is an adjacent\nrecord-format extension requiring separate authorization. Notices remain\n`{fileId, attemptId, visitId, status: \"queued\"}`. Preserve these public interfaces.\n\n## Transfer progress\n\nA file may supply a shared `progressFeed` with a native EventTarget `target`,\n`start()`, and `decode(packet)`. Starting a view of the stream may synchronously\nemit its cached progress event; further progress events arrive on the same target.\nEvents are named `progress` and their detail is decoded to `{seq, loaded}`.\nDecoding may return a value or a Promise. The same file and source can be reused\nby multiple panel visits. Each selected or retried attempt starts one subscription.\n\nThe workspace accepts `recordProgress({fileId, attemptId, frame})` and\n`showProgress({fileId, attemptId, frame})`. The former is a passive retained audit\nsink. Every accepted progress event is recorded exactly once when decoding\nfinishes, including work accepted before the visit closes. A retired visit\naccepts no new progress events. Removing a listener does not retract a decode\nthat already started. Closing or replacing the visit stops visible progress;\nnew visits remain live. `showProgress` may synchronously select or hide through\nthe workspace, and normal current progress must remain visible. Progress does\nnot change queue records, native transfer cancellation, or accepted retry policy.\nDisposal removes subscriptions and cancels transfers; work already accepted\nfor audit remains retained. Preserve these adapter interfaces.\n", "artifact": "inputs/source/README.md", "sha256": "176839cddbe14170b62bfac215547a7f47e5942331f17c95fac37a2c13362f69"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "// A queued notice describes the accepted attempt in its originating visit.\nexport function publishQueued(state, visit, handle) {\n  state.showQueued({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId: handle.attemptId,\n    visitId: visit.id, status: \"queued\" });\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/source/src/notice.js", "sha256": "73e745110bf4f17ec126903f39780c134e0286ac8859939d0f0e3383f944fe56"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\n// A retry click accepts exactly one command for the current visit and file.\nexport function retryTransfer(state) {\n  const visit = state.current;\n  if (!visit || !visit.handle || state.disposed) return null;\n  const previous = visit.handle;\n  previous.cancel();\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"retry\");\n  if (state.current === visit) visit.handle = handle;\n  else handle.cancel();\n  if (state.current === visit) publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  return handle;\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/source/src/retry.js", "sha256": "69f8b3b9e8f1f97f5a66e39a2fdad4c13e0668662ba4f32942e9bed3b2d382ff"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { watchTransferProgress } from \"./progress.js\";\n\nexport function createTransfer(file, attemptId, transport, onCancel, progress = {}) {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  let stopProgress = () => {};\n  controller.signal.addEventListener(\"abort\", () => {\n    stopProgress();\n    onCancel({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  }, { once: true });\n  transport({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  if (file.progressFeed) {\n    stopProgress = watchTransferProgress(file.progressFeed, progress.receive, progress.record);\n    if (controller.signal.aborted) stopProgress();\n  }\n  return { file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal,\n    cancel() { if (!controller.signal.aborted) controller.abort(); } from \"./transfer.js\";\n\n// Queue records are retained even when their originating panel visit ends.\nexport function makeQueueRecord(file, attemptId, visitId, kind) {\n  return { fileId: file.id, attemptId, visitId, kind };\n}\n\nexport function enqueueTransfer(state, visit, kind) {\n  const attemptId = ++state.nextAttempt;\n  state.record(makeQueueRecord(visit.file, attemptId, visit.id, kind));\n  const progress = {\n    record(frame) { state.recordProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame }); },\n    receive(frame) {\n      if (state.current === visit) state.showProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame });\n    },\n  };\n  const handle = createTransfer(visit.file, attemptId, state.transport, state.onCancel, progress);\n  state.tasks.set(attemptId, handle);\n enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\nexport function releaseVisit(visit) {\n  const handle = visit.handle;\n  visit.handle = null;\n  if (handle) handle.cancel();\n}\n\nexport function selectFile(state, file) {\n  if (state.disposed) return null;\n  const requestId = ++state.nextVisit;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n  if (state.disposed || state.nextVisit !== requestId) return null;\n  const visit = { id: requestId, file, handle: null };\n  state.current = visit;\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"select\");\n  if (state.current === visit) {\n    visit.handle = handle;\n    publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  } else {\n    handle.cancel();\n  }\n  return handle;\n}\n\nexport function hidePanel(state) {\n  state.nextVisit++;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n}\n\nexport function disposeWorkspace(state) {\n  if (state.disposed) return;\n  state.disposed = true;\n  hidePanel(state);\n  for (const handle of state.tasks.values()) handle.cancel();\n}\n", "artifact": decoded = feed.decode(event.detail);\n    if (decoded && typeof decoded.then === \"function\") decoded.then(deliver);\n    else deliver(decoded);\n  };\n  feed.start();\n  feed.target.addEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n  return () => feed.target.removeEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/source/src/progress.js", "sha256": No formal test suite is present.\"}", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/delivery.txt", "sha256": "a7100a6192cc0367bc8d7e2de459316fb663798aa4a6a5aa1f0ce00b33314c78"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "// A queued notice describes the accepted attempt in its originating visit.\nexport function publishQueued(state, visit, handle) {\n  state.showQueued({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId: handle.attemptId,\n    visitId: visit.id, status: \"queued\" });\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/notice.js", "sha256": "73e745110bf4f17ec126903f39780c134e0286ac8859939d0f0e3383f944fe56"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\n// A retry click accepts exactly one command for the current visit and file.\nexport function retryTransfer(state) {\n  const visit = state.current;\n  if (!visit || !visit.handle || state.disposed) return null;\n  const previous = visit.handle;\n  previous.cancel();\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"retry\");\n  if (state.current === visit) visit.handle = handle;\n  else handle.cancel();\n  if (state.current === visit) publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  return handle;\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/retry.js", "sha256": "69f8b3b9e8f1f97f5a66e39a2fdad4c13e0668662ba4f32942e9bed3b2d382ff"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { watchTransferProgress } from \"./progress.js\";\n\nexport function createTransfer(file, attemptId, transport, onCancel, progress = {}) {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  let stopProgress = () => {};\n  controller.signal.addEventListener(\"abort\", () => {\n    stopProgress();\n    onCancel({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  }, { once: true });\n  transport({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  if (file.progressFeed) {\n    stopProgress = watchTransferProgress(file.progressFeed, progress.receive, progress.record);\n    if (controller.signal.aborted) stopProgress();\n  }\n  return { file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal,\n    cancel() { if (!controller.signal.aborted) controller.abort(); } };\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/transfer.js", "sha256": "fd27be4d41174cd20fea6d50e5b6be08da089f46779823c699709b64846fc4f8"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { createTransfer } from \"./transfer.js\";\n\n// Queue records are retained even when their originating panel visit ends.\nexport function makeQueueRecord(file, attemptId, visitId, kind) {\n  return { fileId: file.id, attemptId, visitId, kind };\n}\n\nexport function enqueueTransfer(state, visit, kind) {\n  const attemptId = ++state.nextAttempt;\n  state.record(makeQueueRecord(visit.file, attemptId, visit.id, kind));\n  const progress = {\n    record(frame) { state.recordProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame }); },\n    receive(frame) {\n      if (state.current === visit) state.showProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame });\n    },\n  };\n  const handle = createTransfer(visit.file, attemptId, state.transport, state.onCancel, progress);\n  state.tasks.set(attemptId, handle);\n  return handle;\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/queue.js", "sha256": "7018d36249fb316c093c8fd992e8eabb1dc9a5ea82c054107120e007293bce4d"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\nexport function releaseVisit(visit) {\n  const handle = visit.handle;\n  visit.handle = null;\n  if (handle) handle.cancel();\n}\n\nexport function selectFile(state, file) {\n  if (state.disposed) return null;\n  const requestId = ++state.nextVisit;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n  if (state.disposed || state.nextVisit !== requestId) return null;\n  const visit = { id: requestId, file, handle: null };\n  state.current = visit;\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"select\");\n  if (state.current === visit) {\n    visit.handle = handle;\n    publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  } else {\n    handle.cancel();\n  }\n  return handle;\n}\n\nexport function hidePanel(state) {\n  state.nextVisit++;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n}\n\nexport function disposeWorkspace(state) {\n  if (state.disposed) return;\n  state.disposed = true;\n  hidePanel(state);\n  for (const handle of state.tasks.values()) handle.cancel();\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/panel.js", "sha256": "9c0ac97b224ade688d62bde60f1b3327e7b565a22ec4bf16450ae1269190269e"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "// The source is shared by visits of the same file. Decoding may settle later.\nexport function watchTransferProgress(feed, receive, record) {\n  const listener = event => {\n    const deliver = frame => { record(frame); receive(frame); };\n    const decoded = feed.decode(event.detail);\n    if (decoded && typeof decoded.then === \"function\") decoded.then(deliver);\n    else deliver(decoded);\n  };\n  feed.target.addEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n  feed.start();\n  return () => feed.target.removeEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto1/final/src/progress.js", "sha256": "aea7873556d3aa9fc49b4820ad964f472c2d64eab2810fe5fba2616a0f0bdefb"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { selectFile, hidePanel, disposeWorkspace } from \"./panel.js\";\nimport { retryTransfer } from \"./retry.js\";\n\nexport function createTransferWorkspace({ transport, onCancel, record, showQueued, recordProgress = () => {}, showProgress = () => {} }) {\n  const state = { transport, onCancel, record, showQueued, recordProgress, showProgress, current: null,\n    nextVisit: 0, nextAttempt: 0, tasks: new Map(), disposed: false };\n  return {\n    select(file) { return selectFile(state, file); },\n    retry() { return retryTransfer(state); },\n    hide() { hidePanel(state); },\n    dispose() { disposeWorkspace(state); },\n    snapshot() {\n      const visit = state.current;\n      return { visitId: visit ? visit.id : null, fileId: visit ? visit.file.id : null,\n        attemptId: visit && visit.handle ? visit.handle.attemptId : null,\n        disposed: state.disposed, attempts: state.nextAttempt };\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nexport { createTransfer } from \"./transfer.js\";\nexport { makeQueueRecord, enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nexport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\nexport { releaseVisit, selectFile, hidePanel, disposeWorkspace } from \"./panel.js\";\nexport { retryTransfer } from \"./retry.js\";\n", "artifact": feed.target.removeEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n }\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/patch.diff", "sha256": "f76dcadd193f3cf1a35ad90f5478636f44dc3d691f411ad03270f14656cf70ee"}, {"role": "training_public_delivery", "content": "{\"report\":\"Fixed `src/progress.js` by registering the progress listener before starting the feed, restoring synchronous cached/warm progress delivery. Verified with `/runtime/node`: initial cached progress, later streaming, async decode auditing after hide without visibility, and source reuse across visits. No test suite is present; queue-record and other transfer lifecycle code were unchanged.\"}", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/delivery.txt", "sha256": "3f9919145da5ce7cdc74d49da6fe9dc4ae93afd7eec4808c759d9c8f462e9904"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "// A queued notice describes the accepted attempt in its originating visit.\nexport function publishQueued(state, visit, handle) {\n  state.showQueued({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId: handle.attemptId,\n    visitId: visit.id, status: \"queued\" });\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/final/src/notice.js", "sha256": "73e745110bf4f17ec126903f39780c134e0286ac8859939d0f0e3383f944fe56"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\n// A retry click accepts exactly one command for the current visit and file.\nexport function retryTransfer(state) {\n  const visit = state.current;\n  if (!visit || !visit.handle || state.disposed) return null;\n  const previous = visit.handle;\n  previous.cancel();\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"retry\");\n  if (state.current === visit) visit.handle = handle;\n  else handle.cancel();\n  if (state.current === visit) publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  return handle;\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/final/src/retry.js", "sha256": "69f8b3b9e8f1f97f5a66e39a2fdad4c13e0668662ba4f32942e9bed3b2d382ff"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { watchTransferProgress } from \"./progress.js\";\n\nexport function createTransfer(file, attemptId, transport, onCancel, progress = {}) {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  let stopProgress = () => {};\n  controller.signal.addEventListener(\"abort\", () => {\n    stopProgress();\n    onCancel({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  }, { once: true });\n  transport({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  if (file.progressFeed) {\n    stopProgress = watchTransferProgress(file.progressFeed, progress.receive, progress.record);\n    if (controller.signal.aborted) stopProgress();\n  }\n  return { file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal,\n    cancel() { if (!controller.signal.aborted) controller.abort(); } };\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/final/src/transfer.js", "sha256": "fd27be4d41174cd20fea6d50e5b6be08da089f46779823c699709b64846fc4f8"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { createTransfer } from \"./transfer.js\";\n\n// Queue records are retained even when their originating panel visit ends.\nexport function makeQueueRecord(file, attemptId, visitId, kind) {\n  return { fileId: file.id, attemptId, visitId, kind };\n}\n\nexport function enqueueTransfer(state, visit, kind) {\n  const attemptId = ++state.nextAttempt;\n  state.record(makeQueueRecord(visit.file, attemptId, visit.id, kind));\n  const progress = {\n    record(frame) { state.recordProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame }); },\n    receive(frame) {\n      if (state.current === visit) state.showProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame });\n    },\n  };\n  const handle = createTransfer(visit.file, attemptId, state.transport, state.onCancel, progress);\n  state.tasks.set(attemptId, handle);\n  return handle;\n}\n", "artifact": "inputs/auto2/final/src/queue.js", "sha256": "7018d36249fb316c093c8fd992e8eabb1dc9a5ea82c054107120e007293bce4d"}, {"role": "training_source", "content": "import { enqueueTransfer } from \"./queue.js\";\nimport { publishQueued } from \"./notice.js\";\n\nexport function releaseVisit(visit) {\n  const handle = visit.handle;\n  visit.handle = null;\n  if (handle) handle.cancel();\n}\n\nexport function selectFile(state, file) {\n  if (state.disposed) return null;\n  const requestId = ++state.nextVisit;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n  if (state.disposed || state.nextVisit !== requestId) return null;\n  const visit = { id: requestId, file, handle: null };\n  state.current = visit;\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"select\");\n  if (state.current === visit) {\n    visit.handle = handle;\n    publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  } else {\n    handle.cancel();\n  }\n  return handle;\n}\n\nexport function hidePanel(state) {\n  state.nextVisit++;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n}\n\nexport function disposeWorkspace(state) {\n  if (state.disposed) return;\n  state.disposed = true;\n  hidePanel(state);\n  for (const handle of state.tasks.values()) handle.cancel();\n}\n", "artifact": "documented_requirement", "statement": "Restore the initial warm-source update, preserving ordinary streaming and existing transfer/retry/navigation/cleanup/audit behavior; record-format extension is outside scope.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/task.md", "start": 1, "end": 4, "quote": "A transfer with a warm progress source shows no initial progress until a later\npacket arrives. Restore the initial progress update without changing the public\ntransfer, retry, navigation, cleanup, or audit behavior. Preserve the ordinary\nstreaming path and subsequent uses. The queue-record format is outside this fix."}]}, {"id": "R-source-timing", "kind": "documented_requirement", "statement": "Starting the shared progress source can emit synchronously; decoding can settle synchronously or later and the same source can be reused across visits.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/README.md", "start": 45, "end": 50, "quote": "A file may supply a shared `progressFeed` with a native EventTarget `target`,\n`start()`, and `decode(packet)`. Starting a view of the stream may synchronously\nemit its cached progress event; further progress events arrive on the same target.\nEvents are named `progress` and their detail is decoded to `{seq, loaded}`.\nDecoding may return a value or a Promise. The same file and source can be reused\nby multiple panel visits. Each selected or retried attempt starts one subscription."}]}, {"id": "R-history-view", "kind": "documented_requirement", "statement": "Each admitted event must retain exactly one audit at decode completion even after departure; retired visits admit no new events; visible updates stop on closing/replacement; callbacks may reenter.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/README.md", "start": 52, "end": 62, "quote": "The workspace accepts `recordProgress({fileId, attemptId, frame})` and\n`showProgress({fileId, attemptId, frame})`. The former is a passive retained audit\nsink. Every accepted progress event is recorded exactly once when decoding\nfinishes, including work accepted before the visit closes. A retired visit\naccepts no new progress events. Removing a listener does not retract a decode\nthat already started. Closing or replacing the visit stops visible progress;\nnew visits remain live. `showProgress` may synchronously select or hide through\nthe workspace, and normal current progress must remain visible. Progress does\nnot change queue records, native transfer cancellation, or accepted retry policy.\nDisposal removes subscriptions and cancels transfers; work already accepted\nfor audit remains retained. Preserve these adapter interfaces."}]}, {"id": "R-retry-entry", "kind": "documented_requirement", "statement": "An accepted retry is bound at entry, cancellation precedes enqueue, exactly one retained command survives reentrant navigation.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/README.md", "start": 11, "end": 16, "quote": "Retry already cancels the previous transfer before enqueueing an accepted retry. Preserve that behavior during progress maintenance.\nAn accepted retry always belongs to the file and visit present at entry, creates\nexactly one new attempt, and remains in the queue record even if cancellation\ncauses navigation. Attempt IDs increase once per enqueued command within each\nworkspace; navigation must not retarget an accepted retry to the newly selected\nfile or drop its command. Queueing and recording are synchronous."}]}, {"id": "R-reentry", "kind": "documented_requirement", "statement": "Native abort callbacks run synchronously and may navigate or reopen identical data; newer visit retains ownership. Retained retry and cleanup obligations remain.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/README.md", "start": 18, "end": 34, "quote": "Cancellation uses a native `AbortController`: `abort()` synchronously calls the\nhost's `onCancel` observer before returning. That observer may select another\nfile, hide the panel, or hide and reopen the identical file object. New selection\nimmediately starts its own transfer. The newer visit must retain its view and\nhandle when the older retry resumes. Queued notices are visible output: a notice\nfrom a retired visit must not be sent, including after reopening the same file.\nNormal retries in a current visit still issue one queued notice each.\n\nThe accepted old retry must still enqueue and record once after such navigation.\nIts newly created transfer is canceled immediately if its visit has ended; this\ndoes not remove its retained queue record. There must be no remaining active\ntransfer from that old visit. The new visit remains able to retry and hide;\nhiding cancels its owned handle and repeated cancellation is idempotent.\nSelection and hiding may themselves cancel an old handle and synchronously\nreceive newer navigation; the newer navigation keeps ownership. A hidden panel\naccepts no retry. Workspace disposal cancels every remaining transfer and is\nidempotent. Disposing one workspace cannot affect another."}]}, {"id": "R-format", "kind": "documented_requirement", "statement": "Keep public sink and queue interfaces; adjacent record extension needs separate authorization.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/README.md", "start": 36, "end": 41, "quote": "`transport({file, attemptId, signal})` starts a pending physical transfer and\nobserves the native signal. `record` and `showQueued` are passive synchronous\nsinks. Every queue record remains exactly `{fileId, attemptId, visitId, kind}`;\n`kind` is `select` or `retry`. Adding `byteCount` from `file.size` is an adjacent\nrecord-format extension requiring separate authorization. Notices remain\n`{fileId, attemptId, visitId, status: \"queued\"}`. Preserve these public interfaces."}]}, {"id": "F-progress", "kind": "source_fact", "statement": "Current listener delivers to record and view after decode; current source calls source start before adding its listener. This ordering is a current fact, not intended preservation.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/src/progress.js", "start": 1, "end": 12, "quote": "// The source is shared by visits of the same file. Decoding may settle later.\nexport function watchTransferProgress(feed, receive, record) {\n  const listener = event => {\n    const deliver = frame => { record(frame); receive(frame); };\n    const decoded = feed.decode(event.detail);\n    if (decoded && typeof decoded.then === \"function\") decoded.then(deliver);\n    else deliver(decoded);\n  };\n  feed.start();\n  feed.target.addEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n  return () => feed.target.removeEventListener(\"progress\", listener);\n}"}]}, {"id": "F-view-owner", "kind": "source_fact", "statement": "The audit callback captures entry file/attempt, while visible delivery checks current visit identity.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/src/queue.js", "start": 8, "end": 19, "quote": "export function enqueueTransfer(state, visit, kind) {\n  const attemptId = ++state.nextAttempt;\n  state.record(makeQueueRecord(visit.file, attemptId, visit.id, kind));\n  const progress = {\n    record(frame) { state.recordProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame }); },\n    receive(frame) {\n      if (state.current === visit) state.showProgress({ fileId: visit.file.id, attemptId, frame });\n    },\n  };\n  const handle = createTransfer(visit.file, attemptId, state.transport, state.onCancel, progress);\n  state.tasks.set(attemptId, handle);\n  return handle;"}]}, {"id": "F-cancel", "kind": "source_fact", "statement": "Transfer owns a controller and a deferred cleanup callback; its abort invokes cleanup and synchronous cancellation observer. The returned resource remains attempt-local.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/src/transfer.js", "start": 3, "end": 16, "quote": "export function createTransfer(file, attemptId, transport, onCancel, progress = {}) {\n  const controller = new AbortController();\n  let stopProgress = () => {};\n  controller.signal.addEventListener(\"abort\", () => {\n    stopProgress();\n    onCancel({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  }, { once: true });\n  transport({ file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal });\n  if (file.progressFeed) {\n    stopProgress = watchTransferProgress(file.progressFeed, progress.receive, progress.record);\n    if (controller.signal.aborted) stopProgress();\n  }\n  return { file, attemptId, signal: controller.signal,\n    cancel() { if (!controller.signal.aborted) controller.abort(); } };"}]}, {"id": "F-navigation", "kind": "source_fact", "statement": "Selection publishes an identity before enqueue, then checks whether it is still current before assigning the resulting handle; hide invalidates before releasing.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/src/panel.js", "start": 10, "end": 34, "quote": "export function selectFile(state, file) {\n  if (state.disposed) return null;\n  const requestId = ++state.nextVisit;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n  if (state.disposed || state.nextVisit !== requestId) return null;\n  const visit = { id: requestId, file, handle: null };\n  state.current = visit;\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"select\");\n  if (state.current === visit) {\n    visit.handle = handle;\n    publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  } else {\n    handle.cancel();\n  }\n  return handle;\n}\n\nexport function hidePanel(state) {\n  state.nextVisit++;\n  const previous = state.current;\n  state.current = null;\n  if (previous) releaseVisit(previous);\n}"}]}, {"id": "F-retry", "kind": "source_fact", "statement": "Retry captures current visit, cancels prior handle and enqueues for the captured visit; active view identity gates publication.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": [], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/source/src/retry.js", "start": 4, "end": 15, "quote": "// A retry click accepts exactly one command for the current visit and file.\nexport function retryTransfer(state) {\n  const visit = state.current;\n  if (!visit || !visit.handle || state.disposed) return null;\n  const previous = visit.handle;\n  previous.cancel();\n  const handle = enqueueTransfer(state, visit, \"retry\");\n  if (state.current === visit) visit.handle = handle;\n  else handle.cancel();\n  if (state.current === visit) publishQueued(state, visit, handle);\n  return handle;\n}"}]}, {"id": "I-preserve", "kind": "preservation_inference", "statement": "Preservation applies to documented accepted audit/retry histories and visit-owned display/resource semantics; it does not authorize retaining the reported missing-initial-update defect.", "author": "execution_agent_source_review", "related": ["R-history-view", "R-retry-entry", "R-reentry", "F-view-owner"], "anchors": [{"type": "lines", "file": "inputs/task.md", "start": 1, "end": 4, "quote": "A transfer with a warm progress source shows no initial progress until a later\npacket arrives. Restore the initial progress update without changing the public\ntransfer, retry, navigation, cleanup, or audit behavior. Preserve the ordinary\nstreaming path and subsequent uses. The queue-record format is outside this fix."}]}, {"id": "O-auto1-0", "kind": "observed_result", "statement": "Complete observed failing relation and raw checks; not an isolated cause.", "author":
